Now writing the HTTP handler carefully:

```python
class _MCPRequestHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    server_version = 'TVPilotMCP/' + __version__
    protocol_version = 'HTTP/1.1'

    # -- helpers ----------------------------------------------------
    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
        if getattr(self.server, 'verbose', False):
            sys.stderr.write('[mcp-http] ' + (fmt % args) + '\n')

    def _send_json(self, status, obj):
        body = json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':')).encode('utf-8')
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header('Content-Type', 'application/json; charset=utf-8')
        self.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(body)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def _send_empty(self, status):
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header('Content-Length', '0')
        self.end_headers()

    def _target(self):
        parsed = urlparse(self.path)
        return parsed.path.rstrip('/') or '/', parse_qs(parsed.query)
```
Hmm, `/mcp/` → rstrip('/') → '/mcp' ✓; '/' → '' or '/' → '/' ✓.

```python
    def do_POST(self):
        path, query = self._target()
        if path != '/mcp':
            return self._send_json(404, {'error': 'not found'})
        try:
            length = int(self.headers.get('Content-Length') or 0)
        except ValueError:
            length = 0
        raw = self.rfile.read(length) if length > 0 else b''
        try:
            message = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8')) if raw.strip() else None
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError):
            return self._send_json(200, _error_response(None, PARSE_ERROR, 'JSON 解析失败'))
        response = self.server.app.handle(message)
        if response is None:
            return self._send_empty(202)
        session_id = (query.get('sessionId') or [None])[0]
        stream = self.server.sessions.get(session_id) if session_id else None
        if stream is not None:
            try:
                stream.send('message', response)
            except OSError:
                self.server.sessions.pop(session_id, None)
                return self._send_json(200, response)
            return self._send_empty(202)
        self._send_json(200, response)
```
Hmm — `message = None` for empty body → `handle(None)` → `_error_response(None, INVALID_REQUEST, ...)` ✓ (200 + JSON-RPC error). Fine.

```python
    def do_GET(self):
        path, query = self._target()
        if path == '/health':
            return self._send_json(200, {'status': 'ok', 'server': SERVER_NAME, 'version': __version__})
        if path != '/mcp':
            return self._send_json(404, {'error': 'not found'})
        if 'text/event-stream' not in (self.headers.get('Accept') or ''):
            return self._send_json(200, {
                'transport': 'http+sse', 'post': '/mcp',
                'sse': 'GET /mcp（Accept: text/event-stream）',
            })
        self._serve_sse(query)
```

```python
    def _serve_sse(self, query):
        session_id = (query.get('sessionId') or [os.urandom(8).hex()])[0]
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header('Content-Type', 'text/event-stream; charset=utf-8')
        self.send_header('Cache-Control', 'no-cache')
        self.send_header('Connection', 'keep-alive')
        self.end_headers()
        stream = _SSEStream(self.wfile)
        self.server.sessions[session_id] = stream
        host = self.headers.get('Host') or '127.0.0.1'
        try:
            stream.send('endpoint', f'http://{host}/mcp?sessionId={session_id}')
            stop = self.server.stop_event
            while not stop.wait(15):
                stream.send('ping', '{}')
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            pass
        finally:
            self.server.sessions.pop(session_id, None)
            self.close_connection = True
```
✓.

Server class:
```python
class _MCPServerHTTP(ThreadingHTTPServer):
    daemon_threads = True
    allow_reuse_address = True

    def __init__(self, address, app, *, verbose=False):
        self.app = app
        self.verbose = verbose
        self.sessions: Dict[str, _SSEStream] = {}
        self.stop_event = threading.Event()
        super().__init__(address, _MCPRequestHandler)

    def stop(self):
        self.stop_event.set()
        self.shutdown()
        self.server_close()
```

Also `serve_stdio`:
```python
def serve_stdio(app=None, *, stdin=None, stdout=None):
    app = app or MCPServer()
    stdin = sys.stdin if stdin is None else stdin
    stdout = sys.stdout if stdout is None else stdout
    for line in stdin:
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        out = app.handle_line(line)
        if out is not None:
            stdout.write(out + '\n')
            stdout.flush()
    return 0
```
✓.

    def handle_line(self, line):
        try:
            message = json.loads(line)
        except ValueError:
            return json.dumps(_error_response(None, PARSE_ERROR, 'JSON 解析失败'),
                              ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':'))
        response = self.handle(message)
        if response is None:
            return None
        return json.dumps(response, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':'))

Let me write the final code once more in full, carefully, to avoid syntax errors.

```python
"""TVPilot MCP Server：把既有电视控制工具暴露给任意 MCP agent。

* 协议：MCP 2025-03-26（JSON-RPC 2.0），方法 initialize / ping / tools/list / tools/call
* 传输：HTTP+SSE（POST /mcp、GET /mcp 为 SSE 流）与 stdio（``python -m tvpilot.mcp --stdio``）
* 零新依赖：只用标准库（json / http.server / urllib / threading ...）+ tvpilot 既有模块
* 省 token：工具数 <=10、单个描述 <=80 字符、截图只回 URL（默认 240p）、
  结果只留关键字段（去掉 ok/cost_ms 包装、不带 base64）、JSON 紧凑序列化

用法::

    python -m tvpilot.mcp --stdio                # stdio 传输
    python -m tvpilot.mcp --port 8899            # HTTP+SSE 传输（默认 127.0.0.1:8899）
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import inspect
import json
import os
import sys
import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, Mapping, Optional, Sequence, Tuple
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse

from tvpilot import blueprint as _blueprint
from tvpilot import screenshot as _screenshot
from tvpilot import state as _state
from tvpilot import tools as _tools
from tvpilot.errors import ERR_TV_UNREACHABLE
```

Hmm — `from typing import ...` — I use `Dict`, `Any`, `Mapping`, `Optional`, `Tuple`, `Callable`, `Sequence`? Let me include what I use; unused imports are lint noise. I'll use: Any, Callable, Dict, Mapping, Optional, Sequence, Tuple ✓ (Sequence used in `_project` keep arg... let me use `Tuple[str, ...]` or `Sequence[str]` ✓).

Continue:

```python
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# §0 接口面：既有函数（只读复用，不修改这些模块）
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

BlueprintRunner = _blueprint.BlueprintRunner
normalize_tool_result = _blueprint.normalize_tool_result
tool_screenshot = _screenshot.tool_screenshot
get_foreground_pkg = _state.get_foreground_pkg
tool_system_state = _state.tool_system_state
tool_combo_go_home = _tools.tool_combo_go_home
tool_input_text = _tools.tool_input_text
tool_keyevent = _tools.tool_keyevent
tool_launch_app = _tools.tool_launch_app
tool_tap = _tools.tool_tap
tool_zap = _tools.tool_zap

#: 逻辑名 -> 定义模块（配合 _live() 做运行时解析）
_SOURCES: Dict[str, Any] = {
    'BlueprintRunner': _blueprint,
    'tool_screenshot': _screenshot,
    'get_foreground_pkg': _state,
    'tool_system_state': _state,
    'tool_combo_go_home': _tools,
    'tool_input_text': _tools,
    'tool_keyevent': _tools,
    'tool_launch_app': _tools,
    'tool_tap': _tools,
    'tool_zap': _tools,
}
_ORIGINALS: Dict[str, Any] = {name: globals()[name] for name in _SOURCES}


def _live(name: str) -> Any:
    """运行时解析被调用对象（测试注入点，见 §7）。

    两种替换方式都被识别：``tvpilot.mcp.<name> = fake``（调用方常用）或
    ``tvpilot.tools.<name> = fake``（源模块上打桩）；两者都没动时等价于直接调用。
    """
    own = globals().get(name)
    if own is not _ORIGINALS.get(name):
        return own
    return getattr(_SOURCES[name], name, own)
```

```python
def _env_flag(name: str, default: bool) -> bool:
    raw = os.environ.get(name)
    if raw is None:
        return default
    return raw.strip().lower() not in ('', '0', 'false', 'no', 'off')
```

Now, I realize I should double check one thing — `tools/call` result for `isError: False`. My `_tool_result(payload, is_error=False, ...)` → `{'content': [...], 'isError': False, 'structuredContent': {...}}` ✓.

Let me write `_tool_result`:
```python
def _tool_result(payload: Mapping[str, Any], *, is_error: bool = False,
                 error: Optional[str] = None, code: Optional[str] = None,
                 structured: bool = True) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    body: Dict[str, Any] = dict(payload or {})
    if error is not None:
        body['error'] = error
        body['code'] = code or ERR_TOOL_ERROR
    text = json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':'))
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {'content': [{'type': 'text', 'text': text}], 'isError': bool(is_error)}
    if structured:
        result['structuredContent'] = body
    return result
```

```python
def _validate_specs() -> None:
    """模块加载即校验 §2.2 的硬约束（工具数 / 描述长度 / 唯一名 / 覆盖接口面）。"""
    if len(TOOL_SPECS) > MAX_TOOL_COUNT:
        raise RuntimeError(f'工具数 {len(TOOL_SPECS)} 超过上限 {MAX_TOOL_COUNT}')
    names = [spec.name for spec in TOOL_SPECS]
    if len(set(names)) != len(names):
        raise RuntimeError(f'工具名重复: {names}')
    for spec in TOOL_SPECS:
        if len(spec.description) > MAX_TOOL_DESCRIPTION_CHARS:
            raise RuntimeError(f'{spec.name} 描述超长: {len(spec.description)} > {MAX_TOOL_DESCRIPTION_CHARS}')
        if spec.source not in _SOURCES:
            raise RuntimeError(f'{spec.name} 的来源 {spec.source} 不在 §0 接口面里')
        if not callable(spec.handler):
            raise RuntimeError(f'{spec.name} handler 不可调用')
    missing = set(_SOURCES) - {spec.source for spec in TOOL_SPECS}
    if missing:
        raise RuntimeError(f'§0 接口面未被任何工具覆盖: {sorted(missing)}')


_validate_specs()
```

Failure semantics table:

| 层 | 情形 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|
| JSON-RPC | JSON 解析失败 / 非法消息 / 未知方法 / 参数缺失或类型错 / 未知工具 | fail-closed：-32700/-32600/-32601/-32602，无 result |
| 通知 | 任意 notification | 不回包（含出错时）|
| 工具执行 | 既有工具返回 (None, code, msg) | fail-open 到 agent：isError=true，payload{error, code, ...关键字段} |
| 工具执行 | 工具抛异常 | isError=true，code=internal_error |
| 蓝图 | run 结果 ok=false | isError=true + failed_step/error + steps 状态 |
| 蓝图 | 缺 name | fail-open：列出蓝图（不算错误）|
| 前台 | get_foreground_pkg() 为 None | isError=true，code=ERR_TV_UNREACHABLE |
| 截图 | 结果无 URL | isError=true，code=screenshot_failed |
| 传输 | SSE 写失败 | 降级：直接回 POST JSON |

```python
import json, urllib.request

BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:8899/mcp'

def rpc(method, params=None, id=1):
    body = json.dumps({'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'id': id, 'method': method, 'params': params or {}}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE, data=body, headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as resp:
        return json.loads(resp.read())

print(rpc('initialize', {'protocolVersion': '2025-03-26', 'capabilities': {}}))
print(rpc('tools/call', {'name': 'tv_zap', 'arguments': {'channel': '中央一台'}}, id=2))
```

Let me finalize `_invoke`:
```python
def _checked(name: str, raw: Any, keep: Sequence[str] = ()) -> Any:
    data, err_code, err_message = normalize_tool_result(raw)
    if err_code is not None or err_message is not None:
        raise ToolCallError(err_code or ERR_TOOL_ERROR, err_message or f'{name} 调用失败',
                            _project(data, keep))
    return data


def _invoke(name: str, *args: Any, keep: Sequence[str] = (), **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
    """按 §1 契约调用既有工具：(data, err_code, err_message) 三元组拆包，失败抛 ToolCallError。"""
    return _checked(name, _live(name)(*args, **kwargs), keep)
```
✓.

```python
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 结果塑形（§0 响应字段约束：只回关键字段、去 ok/cost_ms 包装、不带 base64）
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

def _project(data: Any, keep: Sequence[str] = ()) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """把工具返回的 data 压成关键字段。

    * ``keep`` 命中 → 只留白名单字段（形状稳定，省 token）；
    * 一条都没命中 → 退化为"丢噪声字段"的透传（工具返回结构未知时的兜底）。
    """
    if not isinstance(data, Mapping):
        return {} if data is None else {'value': data}
    picked = {key: data[key] for key in keep if key in data}
    if picked:
        return picked
    return {key: value for key, value in data.items()
            if key not in _NOISE_KEYS and value is not None}
```

Regarding length: I'll keep prose sections compact and let the code speak.</think> # TVPilot MCP Server —— 设计 + 代码 + 单测 + 使用示例

先给结论：新增 **1 个实现文件 + 1 个测试文件**，四个冻结模块一个字节都不动，全部通过 `import` 只读复用。工具收敛为 **10 个**（正好把 §0 接口面列出的 10 个既有函数一对一覆盖），JSON-RPC 2.0 自实现，传输支持 **HTTP+SSE（/mcp）与 stdio**，全程零新依赖。

---

## 1 · 设计

### 1.1 爆炸半径对照（§0 缺口 → 方案 → 验收项）

| 缺口 | 方案 | 验收项 |
|---|---|---|
| HTTP/SSE + stdio | `http.server` 自写 `/mcp`（POST 收 JSON-RPC、GET 出 SSE 流）；`--stdio` 走行式 JSON-RPC | #8 |
| MCP 2025-03-26 | 自实现 `initialize / ping / tools/list / tools/call` + 通知静默，错误码走 JSON-RPC 标准值 | #2 |
| 工具 ≤10 | 20+ API 收敛为 10 个，**一一对应 §0 的 10 个既有函数**，模块加载即自检（超 10 个直接 `RuntimeError`） | #3 |
| 描述 ≤80 字符 | 描述全部 ≤80 **字符**（顺带保证 ≤80 **字节**），自检 + 单测双重卡 | #3 |
| 截图回 URL 不回 base64 | 响应按白名单投影为 `{"url","width"}`，默认按 240p（426×240）请求 | #5 |
| 去 ok/cost_ms 包装 | 白名单投影 + 噪声字段黑名单（`ok/cost_ms/base64/...` 一律丢） | #4 |
| 零新依赖 | 只 import 标准库 + `tvpilot.*`，单测用 `ast` 扫 import 根 | #9 |
| 现有函数 | `tvpilot.{tools,state,screenshot,blueprint}` 只读复用，`(data, err_code, err_message)` 三元组契约原样沿用 | #6 #7 |

### 1.2 工具表（10 个 = §0 接口面全覆盖）

| 工具 | 调用的既有函数 | 参数 | 成功返回（只留关键字段） |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tv_foreground` | `state.get_foreground_pkg` | — | `package` |
| `tv_state` | `state.tool_system_state` | — | 去噪声透传（结构未内联，见 §5-d） |
| `tv_screenshot` | `screenshot.tool_screenshot` | `width/height`（默认 426/240） | `url, width` |
| `tv_zap` | `tools.tool_zap` | `channel` | `channel, channel_no` |
| `tv_key` | `tools.tool_keyevent` | `key` | `key, channel` |
| `tv_launch` | `tools.tool_launch_app` | `package, activity?` | `package, foreground` |
| `tv_text` | `tools.tool_input_text` | `text` | `text, method` |
| `tv_tap` | `tools.tool_tap` | `x, y` | `x, y` |
| `tv_home` | `tools.tool_combo_go_home` | — | `is_home, foreground_after, warning` |
| `tv_blueprint_run` | `blueprint.BlueprintRunner` | `name?, params?` | `name, steps[{step,status}], failed_step?, error?` |

参数名与 §1 里既有函数的形参名**逐字对齐**（`channel/key/package/activity/text/x/y`），`BlueprintRunner` 只用契约方法 `register_builtins() / list() / get() / run(name, params)`。`ToolSpec.source` 字段记录映射关系，模块加载时校验"每个接口面函数恰好被一个工具覆盖"。

### 1.3 协议与传输行为

- `initialize` → `{"protocolVersion":"2025-03-26","capabilities":{"tools":true},"serverInfo":{...},"instructions":"..."}`；`notifications/initialized` 等通知**不回包**。
- `tools/call` 成功 → `{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"<紧凑 JSON>"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{...}}`（`text` 即验收里说的那个 payload）。
- `POST /mcp`：无 SSE 会话 → `200` + JSON-RPC 响应体（curl/脚本直连）；有 SSE 会话 → `202` + `event: message` 推到流上（老式 HTTP+SSE 客户端语义）。
- `GET /mcp`（`Accept: text/event-stream`）→ `event: endpoint`（给 POST URI + sessionId）+ 15s 心跳；`GET /health` 探活。

### 1.4 失败 / 降级语义

| 层 | 情形 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|
| JSON-RPC | JSON 解析失败 | fail-closed `-32700` |
| JSON-RPC | 非法消息 / 空批量 | `-32600` |
| JSON-RPC | 未知方法（含 `resources/*`、`prompts/*`） | `-32601`（未声明这些 capability） |
| JSON-RPC | 未知工具 / arguments 非对象 / 必填缺失 / 类型不对 | `-32602` |
| JSON-RPC | 通知（无 `id`）出错 | 一律不回包 |
| 工具执行 | 既有工具回 `(None, code, msg)` | fail-open 到 agent：`isError=true`，payload `{白名单字段, error, code}`，agent 可读可重试 |
| 工具执行 | 工具抛异常 | `isError=true`，`code=internal_error` |
| 前台 | `get_foreground_pkg()` 为 `None` | `isError=true`，`code=ERR_TV_UNREACHABLE` |
| 截图 | 结果里找不到 URL | `isError=true`，`code=screenshot_failed` |
| 蓝图 | `BlueprintResult.ok=false` | `isError=true` + `failed_step/error/steps`；**`ok` 不进 payload**（由 `isError` 承担） |
| 蓝图 | 缺 `name` | fail-open：列出可用蓝图，不算错误 |
| 传输 | SSE 写失败（客户端断开） | 降级：本次响应直接回 POST JSON |
| 工具内部 | `tool_keyevent` / `tool_launch_app` 的 ADB→6095 降级 | 不动它们的逻辑，MCP 层保留 `channel` 字段让 agent 看见降级发生了 |

---

## 2 · 代码：`tvpilot/mcp.py`

```python
"""TVPilot MCP Server：把既有电视控制工具暴露给任意 MCP agent（JSON-RPC 2.0）。

* 协议：MCP 2025-03-26 —— initialize / ping / tools/list / tools/call
* 传输：HTTP+SSE（POST /mcp、GET /mcp 为 SSE 流）与 stdio（``python -m tvpilot.mcp --stdio``）
* 零新依赖：只用标准库（json/http.server/urllib/threading/...）+ tvpilot 既有模块
* 省 token：工具数 <=10、单个描述 <=80 字符、截图只回 URL（默认 240p）、
  结果只留关键字段（去掉 ok/cost_ms 包装、绝不带 base64）、紧凑 JSON 序列化

用法::

    python -m tvpilot.mcp --stdio              # stdio 传输
    python -m tvpilot.mcp --port 8899          # HTTP+SSE（默认 127.0.0.1:8899）
    python -m tvpilot.mcp --strict-caps        # capabilities.tools 输出 {listChanged:false}
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import inspect
import json
import os
import sys
import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, Mapping, Optional, Sequence, Tuple
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse

from tvpilot import blueprint as _blueprint
from tvpilot import screenshot as _screenshot
from tvpilot import state as _state
from tvpilot import tools as _tools
from tvpilot.errors import ERR_TV_UNREACHABLE

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# §0 接口面：既有函数只读复用（不修改 tools/state/screenshot/blueprint）
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

BlueprintRunner = _blueprint.BlueprintRunner
normalize_tool_result = _blueprint.normalize_tool_result
tool_screenshot = _screenshot.tool_screenshot
get_foreground_pkg = _state.get_foreground_pkg
tool_system_state = _state.tool_system_state
tool_combo_go_home = _tools.tool_combo_go_home
tool_input_text = _tools.tool_input_text
tool_keyevent = _tools.tool_keyevent
tool_launch_app = _tools.tool_launch_app
tool_tap = _tools.tool_tap
tool_zap = _tools.tool_zap

#: 逻辑名 -> 定义模块（配合 _live() 做运行时解析）
_SOURCES: Dict[str, Any] = {
    'BlueprintRunner': _blueprint,
    'tool_screenshot': _screenshot,
    'get_foreground_pkg': _state,
    'tool_system_state': _state,
    'tool_combo_go_home': _tools,
    'tool_input_text': _tools,
    'tool_keyevent': _tools,
    'tool_launch_app': _tools,
    'tool_tap': _tools,
    'tool_zap': _tools,
}
_ORIGINALS: Dict[str, Any] = {name: globals()[name] for name in _SOURCES}


def _live(name: str) -> Any:
    """运行时解析被调用对象（测试注入点，见 §5-b）。

    两种打桩方式都被识别：``tvpilot.mcp.<name> = fake``（"哪里用就 patch 哪里"）
    或 ``tvpilot.tools.<name> = fake``（"哪里定义就 patch 哪里"）；两者都没动时
    与直接调用完全等价。
    """
    own = globals().get(name)
    if own is not _ORIGINALS.get(name):
        return own
    return getattr(_SOURCES[name], name, own)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 常量
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

__version__ = '1.0.0'

SERVER_NAME = 'tvpilot-mcp-server'
PROTOCOL_VERSION = '2025-03-26'
SUPPORTED_PROTOCOL_VERSIONS: Tuple[str, ...] = (PROTOCOL_VERSION,)

MAX_TOOL_COUNT = 10                 # §2.2 硬约束
MAX_TOOL_DESCRIPTION_CHARS = 80     # §2.2 硬约束
MAX_TOOLS_JSON_CHARS = 2000         # tools/list 体积预算（省 token）

DEFAULT_SHOT_WIDTH = 426            # 240p @ 16:9
DEFAULT_SHOT_HEIGHT = 240

JSONRPC_VERSION = '2.0'
PARSE_ERROR = -32700
INVALID_REQUEST = -32600
METHOD_NOT_FOUND = -32601
INVALID_PARAMS = -32602
INTERNAL_ERROR = -32603

ERR_TOOL_ERROR = 'tool_error'
ERR_INTERNAL = 'internal_error'
ERR_SCREENSHOT_FAILED = 'screenshot_failed'
ERR_BLUEPRINT_FAILED = 'blueprint_failed'

#: 响应里一律丢弃的包装/大字段（§0「去掉 ok/cost_ms 包装」「不返回 base64」）
_NOISE_KEYS = frozenset({
    'ok', 'cost', 'cost_ms', 'elapsed', 'elapsed_ms', 'duration_ms', 'warmed',
    'base64', 'image_base64', 'img_base64', 'screenshot_base64', 'raw',
})

DEFAULT_INSTRUCTIONS = '电视控制：换台 tv_zap、按键 tv_key、截图 tv_screenshot（只回 URL）；看不清界面先截图。'


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 异常
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

class _MethodNotFound(Exception):
    def __init__(self, method: str) -> None:
        self.method = method
        super().__init__(method)


class _InvalidParams(Exception):
    """参数不符合工具契约 → JSON-RPC -32602。"""


class ToolCallError(Exception):
    """工具执行期失败 → tools/call 结果里的 isError=True。

    :param code: 错误码（沿用 tvpilot.errors 既有码，或本模块的 ERR_*）
    :param message: 给 agent 看的错误说明
    :param payload: 与成功结果同口径的关键字段（白名单投影）
    """

    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str,
                 payload: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None) -> None:
        self.code = code or ERR_TOOL_ERROR
        self.message = message or '工具调用失败'
        self.payload = dict(payload or {})
        super().__init__(self.message)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 结果塑形（只回关键字段 / 去包装 / 无 base64）
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

def _project(data: Any, keep: Sequence[str] = ()) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """把工具返回的 data 压成关键字段。

    * ``keep`` 命中 → 只留白名单字段（形状稳定、省 token，含 None 也保留）；
    * 一条都没命中 → 退化为"丢噪声字段"的透传（工具返回结构未知时的兜底）。
    """
    if not isinstance(data, Mapping):
        return {} if data is None else {'value': data}
    picked = {key: data[key] for key in keep if key in data}
    if picked:
        return picked
    return {key: value for key, value in data.items()
            if key not in _NOISE_KEYS and value is not None}


def _as_mapping(obj: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """把 Mapping / dataclass / 结果对象（to_dict）统一成 dict。"""
    if isinstance(obj, Mapping):
        return dict(obj)
    to_dict = getattr(obj, 'to_dict', None)
    if callable(to_dict):
        out = to_dict()
        return dict(out) if isinstance(out, Mapping) else {}
    fields = getattr(obj, '__dataclass_fields__', None)
    if isinstance(fields, Mapping):
        return {key: getattr(obj, key) for key in fields}
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key in ('name', 'step', 'status', 'ok', 'error', 'failed_step',
                'steps', 'params', 'description'):
        if hasattr(obj, key):
            out[key] = getattr(obj, key)
    return out


def _tool_result(payload: Mapping[str, Any], *, is_error: bool = False,
                 error: Optional[str] = None, code: Optional[str] = None,
                 structured: bool = True) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """构造 tools/call 结果：content[0].text 是紧凑 JSON，另附 structuredContent。"""
    body: Dict[str, Any] = dict(payload or {})
    if error is not None:
        body['error'] = error
        body['code'] = code or ERR_TOOL_ERROR
    text = json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':'))
    result: Dict[str, Any] = {
        'content': [{'type': 'text', 'text': text}],
        'isError': bool(is_error),
    }
    if structured:
        result['structuredContent'] = body
    return result


def _error_response(msg_id: Any, code: int, message: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {'jsonrpc': JSONRPC_VERSION, 'id': msg_id,
            'error': {'code': code, 'message': message}}


def _result_response(msg_id: Any, result: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {'jsonrpc': JSONRPC_VERSION, 'id': msg_id, 'result': result}


def _compact(obj: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':'))


def _env_flag(name: str, default: bool) -> bool:
    raw = os.environ.get(name)
    if raw is None:
        return default
    return raw.strip().lower() not in ('', '0', 'false', 'no', 'off')


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 参数校验（结构性问题 → -32602；语义问题交给既有工具自己的校验）
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

def _req_str(args: Mapping[str, Any], key: str) -> str:
    val = args.get(key)
    if isinstance(val, str) and val.strip():
        return val
    raise _InvalidParams(f'{key} 必须是非空字符串')


def _opt_str(args: Mapping[str, Any], key: str) -> Optional[str]:
    val = args.get(key)
    if val is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(val, str) and val.strip():
        return val
    raise _InvalidParams(f'{key} 必须是非空字符串')


def _coerce_int(val: Any, key: str) -> int:
    if isinstance(val, bool) or not isinstance(val, (int, float, str)):
        raise _InvalidParams(f'{key} 必须是整数')
    try:
        num = int(val)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        raise _InvalidParams(f'{key} 必须是整数')
    if isinstance(val, float) and not float(val).is_integer():
        raise _InvalidParams(f'{key} 必须是整数')
    return num


def _req_int(args: Mapping[str, Any], key: str) -> int:
    if key not in args or args[key] is None:
        raise _InvalidParams(f'缺少参数: {key}')
    return _coerce_int(args[key], key)


def _opt_int(args: Mapping[str, Any], key: str, default: int,
             lo: int, hi: int) -> int:
    val = args.get(key)
    num = default if val is None else _coerce_int(val, key)
    if not (lo <= num <= hi):
        raise _InvalidParams(f'{key} 超出范围 [{lo}, {hi}]: {num}')
    return num


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 既有工具调用（三元组契约拆包）
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

def _checked(name: str, raw: Any, keep: Sequence[str] = ()) -> Any:
    data, err_code, err_message = normalize_tool_result(raw)
    if err_code is not None or err_message is not None:
        raise ToolCallError(err_code or ERR_TOOL_ERROR,
                            err_message or f'{name} 调用失败',
                            _project(data, keep))
    return data


def _invoke(name: str, *args: Any, keep: Sequence[str] = (), **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
    """按 §1 契约调用既有工具：``(data, err_code, err_message)`` 三元组拆包。

    二元组不当失败解释（沿用 ``blueprint.normalize_tool_result`` 的语义）。
    """
    return _checked(name, _live(name)(*args, **kwargs), keep)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 截图（tool_screenshot 的真实签名未内联，做签名自适应；见 §5-a）
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

_URL_KEYS = ('url', 'screenshot_url', 'image_url', 'href', 'uri', 'path')
_WIDTH_KEYS = ('width', 'w', 'image_width')


def _call_tool_screenshot(width: int, height: int) -> Any:
    """自适应调用 tool_screenshot：只在它确实声明了 width/height 时才传。"""
    fn = _live('tool_screenshot')
    try:
        params = inspect.signature(fn).parameters
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return fn()
    var_kw = any(p.kind is inspect.Parameter.VAR_KEYWORD for p in params.values())
    kwargs: Dict[str, int] = {}
    for key, value in (('width', width), ('height', height)):
        if var_kw or key in params:
            kwargs[key] = value
    return fn(**kwargs)


def _extract_url(data: Any) -> Optional[str]:
    if isinstance(data, str):
        return data.strip() or None
    if isinstance(data, Mapping):
        for key in _URL_KEYS:
            val = data.get(key)
            if isinstance(val, str) and val.strip():
                return val.strip()
    return None


def _extract_width(data: Any, fallback: int) -> int:
    if isinstance(data, Mapping):
        for key in _WIDTH_KEYS:
            val = data.get(key)
            if isinstance(val, bool):
                continue
            if isinstance(val, (int, float)) and val > 0:
                return int(val)
            if isinstance(val, str) and val.isdigit() and int(val) > 0:
                return int(val)
    return int(fallback)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 蓝图结果瘦身
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

def _blueprint_catalogue(runner: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    lister = getattr(runner, 'list', None)
    names = list(lister() or []) if callable(lister) else []
    getter = getattr(runner, 'get', None)
    for bp_name in names:
        item: Dict[str, Any] = {'name': bp_name}
        if callable(getter):
            describe = getattr(getter(bp_name), 'describe', None)
            info = _as_mapping(describe()) if callable(describe) else {}
            if info.get('description'):
                item['description'] = info['description']
            if info.get('params'):
                item['params'] = info['params']
        items.append(item)
    return {'blueprints': items}


def _blueprint_summary(result: Any, name: str) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], bool]:
    """BlueprintResult -> 轻量 payload（name/steps 状态/失败定位）+ 是否成功。"""
    data = _as_mapping(result)
    payload: Dict[str, Any] = {'name': data.get('name') or name}
    steps: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for raw_step in (data.get('steps') or []):
        step = _as_mapping(raw_step)
        status = step.get('status')
        if not status:
            status = 'ok' if step.get('ok', True) else 'failed'
        steps.append({'step': step.get('step') or step.get('name') or '',
                      'status': status})
    payload['steps'] = steps
    ok = bool(data.get('ok', True)) and not data.get('failed_step') and not data.get('error')
    if not ok:
        if data.get('failed_step'):
            payload['failed_step'] = data['failed_step']
        payload['error'] = data.get('error') or f'蓝图 "{name}" 执行失败'
    return payload, ok


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 10 个工具的处理器
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

def _h_foreground(args: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    raw = _live('get_foreground_pkg')()
    pkg = (raw.get('package') or raw.get('pkg')) if isinstance(raw, Mapping) else raw
    if not isinstance(pkg, str) or not pkg.strip():
        raise ToolCallError(ERR_TV_UNREACHABLE, '无法获取前台包名，电视可能离线')
    return {'package': pkg}


def _h_state(args: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return _project(_invoke('tool_system_state'), ())


def _h_screenshot(args: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    width = _opt_int(args, 'width', DEFAULT_SHOT_WIDTH, 16, 7680)
    height = _opt_int(args, 'height', DEFAULT_SHOT_HEIGHT, 16, 4320)
    keep = ('url', 'width')
    data = _checked('tool_screenshot',
                    _call_tool_screenshot(width, height), keep)
    url = _extract_url(data)
    if not url:
        raise ToolCallError(ERR_SCREENSHOT_FAILED,
                            '截图结果里找不到 url（本层不返回 base64）',
                            _project(data, keep))
    return {'url': url, 'width': _extract_width(data, width)}


def _h_zap(args: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    keep = ('channel', 'channel_no')
    return _project(_invoke('tool_zap', _req_str(args, 'channel'), keep=keep), keep)


def _h_key(args: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    keep = ('key', 'channel')
    return _project(_invoke('tool_keyevent', _req_str(args, 'key'), keep=keep), keep)


def _h_launch(args: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    keep = ('package', 'foreground')
    package = _req_str(args, 'package')
    activity = _opt_str(args, 'activity')
    if activity is None:
        data = _invoke('tool_launch_app', package, keep=keep)
    else:
        data = _invoke('tool_launch_app', package, activity, keep=keep)
    return _project(data, keep)


def _h_text(args: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    keep = ('text', 'method')
    text = args.get('text')
    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text:
        raise _InvalidParams('text 必须是非空字符串')
    return _project(_invoke('tool_input_text', text, keep=keep), keep)


def _h_tap(args: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    keep = ('x', 'y')
    return _project(_invoke('tool_tap', _req_int(args, 'x'), _req_int(args, 'y'),
                            keep=keep), keep)


def _h_home(args: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    keep = ('is_home', 'foreground_after', 'warning')
    return _project(_invoke('tool_combo_go_home', keep=keep), keep)


def _h_blueprint(args: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    name = _opt_str(args, 'name')
    params = args.get('params') or {}
    if not isinstance(params, Mapping):
        raise _InvalidParams('params 必须是对象')
    runner = _live('BlueprintRunner')()
    register = getattr(runner, 'register_builtins', None)
    if callable(register):
        register()
    if not name:
        return _blueprint_catalogue(runner)          # 缺 name → 列表（fail-open）
    payload, ok = _blueprint_summary(runner.run(name, dict(params)), name)
    if not ok:
        raise ToolCallError(ERR_BLUEPRINT_FAILED,
                            payload.get('error') or f'蓝图 "{name}" 执行失败', payload)
    return payload


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 工具注册表（frozen dataclass 元组 + dict 索引）
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ToolSpec:
    name: str
    description: str
    source: str                       # §0 接口面里的被调函数（审计/自检用，不出现在 MCP 报文）
    input_schema: Mapping[str, Any]
    handler: Callable[[Mapping[str, Any]], Dict[str, Any]]

    def to_mcp(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {'name': self.name, 'description': self.description,
                'inputSchema': dict(self.input_schema)}


_EMPTY_SCHEMA: Dict[str, Any] = {'type': 'object', 'properties': {}}

TOOL_SPECS: Tuple[ToolSpec, ...] = (
    ToolSpec('tv_foreground', '返回当前前台应用包名', 'get_foreground_pkg',
             _EMPTY_SCHEMA, _h_foreground),
    ToolSpec('tv_state', '返回电视系统状态（前台/屏幕/网络/播放）', 'tool_system_state',
             _EMPTY_SCHEMA, _h_state),
    ToolSpec('tv_screenshot', '截屏返回图片URL，默认240p，不含图片数据', 'tool_screenshot',
             {'type': 'object', 'properties': {
                 'width': {'type': 'integer', 'default': DEFAULT_SHOT_WIDTH},
                 'height': {'type': 'integer', 'default': DEFAULT_SHOT_HEIGHT}}},
             _h_screenshot),
    ToolSpec('tv_zap', '按频道名或台号换台（mytv）', 'tool_zap',
             {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'channel': {'type': 'string'}},
              'required': ['channel']}, _h_zap),
    ToolSpec('tv_key', '发送遥控按键，key 需为 KEYCODE_XXX', 'tool_keyevent',
             {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'key': {'type': 'string'}},
              'required': ['key']}, _h_key),
    ToolSpec('tv_launch', '启动电视应用，可指定 Activity', 'tool_launch_app',
             {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'package': {'type': 'string'},
                                               'activity': {'type': 'string'}},
              'required': ['package']}, _h_launch),
    ToolSpec('tv_text', '向当前焦点输入文字，支持中文', 'tool_input_text',
             {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'text': {'type': 'string'}},
              'required': ['text']}, _h_text),
    ToolSpec('tv_tap', '按坐标点击屏幕 (x, y)', 'tool_tap',
             {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'x': {'type': 'integer'},
                                               'y': {'type': 'integer'}},
              'required': ['x', 'y']}, _h_tap),
    ToolSpec('tv_home', '回到电视桌面并校验前台', 'tool_combo_go_home',
             _EMPTY_SCHEMA, _h_home),
    ToolSpec('tv_blueprint_run', '执行蓝图；缺省 name 时列出可用蓝图', 'BlueprintRunner',
             {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'name': {'type': 'string'},
                                               'params': {'type': 'object'}}},
             _h_blueprint),
)

#: name -> spec 的 O(1) 索引
TOOL_INDEX: Dict[str, ToolSpec] = {spec.name: spec for spec in TOOL_SPECS}


def _validate_specs() -> None:
    """模块加载即校验 §2.2 硬约束与接口面覆盖（违反直接起不来）。"""
    if len(TOOL_SPECS) > MAX_TOOL_COUNT:
        raise RuntimeError(f'工具数 {len(TOOL_SPECS)} 超过上限 {MAX_TOOL_COUNT}')
    names = [spec.name for spec in TOOL_SPECS]
    if len(set(names)) != len(names):
        raise RuntimeError(f'工具名重复: {names}')
    for spec in TOOL_SPECS:
        if len(spec.description) > MAX_TOOL_DESCRIPTION_CHARS:
            raise RuntimeError(f'{spec.name} 描述超长: {len(spec.description)}')
        if spec.source not in _SOURCES:
            raise RuntimeError(f'{spec.name} 的来源 {spec.source} 不在 §0 接口面里')
        if not callable(spec.handler):
            raise RuntimeError(f'{spec.name} 的 handler 不可调用')
    missing = set(_SOURCES) - {spec.source for spec in TOOL_SPECS}
    if missing:
        raise RuntimeError(f'§0 接口面未被任何工具覆盖: {sorted(missing)}')


_validate_specs()


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# JSON-RPC / MCP 处理核心（传输无关）
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

class MCPServer:
    """MCP 请求处理器：``handle()`` 进 JSON-RPC 消息、出 JSON-RPC 响应（通知返回 None）。

    :param strict_caps: ``capabilities.tools`` 输出 ``{"listChanged": False}``
                        （严格 SDK 客户端用；默认输出 ``true``，见 §5-c）
    :param structured_content: 结果是否附带 ``structuredContent``（默认开）
    :param instructions: initialize 里的使用提示（可设空串关闭）
    """

    def __init__(self, *, strict_caps: Optional[bool] = None,
                 structured_content: Optional[bool] = None,
                 instructions: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        self.strict_caps = (_env_flag('TVPILOT_MCP_STRICT_CAPS', False)
                            if strict_caps is None else bool(strict_caps))
        self.structured_content = (_env_flag('TVPILOT_MCP_STRUCTURED_CONTENT', True)
                                   if structured_content is None
                                   else bool(structured_content))
        self.instructions = (DEFAULT_INSTRUCTIONS
                             if instructions is None else instructions)

    # ---- JSON-RPC ------------------------------------------------------ #
    def handle(self, message: Any) -> Any:
        """处理一条 JSON-RPC 消息（或批量列表）；通知返回 None。"""
        if isinstance(message, list):
            if not message:
                return _error_response(None, INVALID_REQUEST, '批量消息不能为空')
            out = [resp for resp in (self.handle(item) for item in message)
                   if resp is not None]
            return out or None
        if not isinstance(message, Mapping):
            return _error_response(None, INVALID_REQUEST, '消息必须是 JSON 对象')

        has_id = 'id' in message
        msg_id = message.get('id')
        method = message.get('method')
        if message.get('jsonrpc') != JSONRPC_VERSION or not isinstance(method, str) or not method:
            return (_error_response(msg_id, INVALID_REQUEST, '非法 JSON-RPC 2.0 消息')
                    if has_id else None)
        params = message.get('params')
        params = {} if params is None else params
        if not isinstance(params, Mapping):
            return (_error_response(msg_id, INVALID_PARAMS, 'params 必须是对象')
                    if has_id else None)

        try:
            result = self.dispatch(method, params)
        except _MethodNotFound as exc:
            return (_error_response(msg_id, METHOD_NOT_FOUND, f'不支持的方法: {exc.method}')
                    if has_id else None)
        except _InvalidParams as exc:
            return (_error_response(msg_id, INVALID_PARAMS, str(exc))
                    if has_id else None)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - 服务端异常收敛为 internal error
            return (_error_response(msg_id, INTERNAL_ERROR, f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}')
                    if has_id else None)
        if not has_id:
            return None
        return _result_response(msg_id, result)

    def handle_line(self, line: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """stdio 用：一行 JSON 进、一行 JSON 出（通知返回 None）。"""
        try:
            message = json.loads(line)
        except ValueError:
            return _compact(_error_response(None, PARSE_ERROR, 'JSON 解析失败'))
        response = self.handle(message)
        return None if response is None else _compact(response)

    def dispatch(self, method: str, params: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Any:
        if method == 'initialize':
            return self.initialize(params)
        if method in ('ping', 'notifications/initialized', 'initialized',
                      'notifications/cancelled'):
            return {}
        if method == 'tools/list':
            return self.tools_list()
        if method == 'tools/call':
            return self.tools_call(params)
        raise _MethodNotFound(method)

    # ---- MCP ----------------------------------------------------------- #
    def initialize(self, params: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        client_version = params.get('protocolVersion')
        version = (client_version if client_version in SUPPORTED_PROTOCOL_VERSIONS
                   else PROTOCOL_VERSION)
        tools_cap: Any = {'listChanged': False} if self.strict_caps else True
        result: Dict[str, Any] = {
            'protocolVersion': version,
            'capabilities': {'tools': tools_cap},
            'serverInfo': {'name': SERVER_NAME, 'version': __version__},
        }
        if self.instructions:
            result['instructions'] = self.instructions
        return result

    def tools_list(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {'tools': [spec.to_mcp() for spec in TOOL_SPECS]}

    def tools_call(self, params: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        name = params.get('name')
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip():
            raise _InvalidParams('缺少工具名 name')
        return self.call_tool(name.strip(), params.get('arguments') or {})

    def call_tool(self, name: str, arguments: Any = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """执行工具并返回 MCP CallToolResult（工具失败是 isError 结果，不是异常）。"""
        spec = TOOL_INDEX.get(name)
        if spec is None:
            raise _InvalidParams(f'未知工具: {name}')
        args = {} if arguments is None else arguments
        if not isinstance(args, Mapping):
            raise _InvalidParams('arguments 必须是对象')
        try:
            payload = spec.handler(args)
        except ToolCallError as exc:
            return _tool_result(exc.payload, is_error=True, error=exc.message,
                                code=exc.code, structured=self.structured_content)
        except _InvalidParams:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - 既有工具异常一律收敛为 isError
            return _tool_result({}, is_error=True,
                                error=f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}',
                                code=ERR_INTERNAL,
                                structured=self.structured_content)
        return _tool_result(payload if isinstance(payload, Mapping) else {'value': payload},
                            is_error=False, structured=self.structured_content)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 传输一：stdio（newline-delimited JSON-RPC）
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

def serve_stdio(app: Optional[MCPServer] = None, *, stdin: Any = None,
                stdout: Any = None) -> int:
    """stdio 主循环：一行一个 JSON-RPC 消息，通知不回包，EOF 退出。"""
    app = app or MCPServer()
    stdin = sys.stdin if stdin is None else stdin
    stdout = sys.stdout if stdout is None else stdout
    for line in stdin:
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        out = app.handle_line(line)
        if out is not None:
            stdout.write(out + '\n')
            stdout.flush()
    return 0


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 传输二：HTTP+SSE（POST /mcp、GET /mcp）
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

class _SSEStream:
    """一条 SSE 连接上的事件出口。"""

    def __init__(self, wfile: Any) -> None:
        self._wfile = wfile
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def send(self, event: str, data: Any) -> None:
        payload = data if isinstance(data, str) else _compact(data)
        chunk = f'event: {event}\ndata: {payload}\n\n'.encode('utf-8')
        with self._lock:
            self._wfile.write(chunk)
            self._wfile.flush()


class _MCPRequestHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    server_version = 'TVPilotMCP/' + __version__
    protocol_version = 'HTTP/1.1'

    def log_message(self, fmt: str, *args: Any) -> None:
        if getattr(self.server, 'verbose', False):
            sys.stderr.write('[mcp-http] ' + (fmt % args) + '\n')

    # -- helpers ------------------------------------------------------- #
    def _target(self) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, list]]:
        parsed = urlparse(self.path)
        return parsed.path.rstrip('/') or '/', parse_qs(parsed.query)

    def _send_json(self, status: int, obj: Any) -> None:
        body = _compact(obj).encode('utf-8')
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header('Content-Type', 'application/json; charset=utf-8')
        self.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(body)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def _send_empty(self, status: int) -> None:
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header('Content-Length', '0')
        self.end_headers()

    # -- verbs ---------------------------------------------------------- #
    def do_POST(self) -> None:
        path, query = self._target()
        if path != '/mcp':
            return self._send_json(404, {'error': 'not found'})
        try:
            length = int(self.headers.get('Content-Length') or 0)
        except ValueError:
            length = 0
        raw = self.rfile.read(length) if length > 0 else b''
        try:
            message = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8')) if raw.strip() else None
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError):
            return self._send_json(200, _error_response(None, PARSE_ERROR, 'JSON 解析失败'))

        response = self.server.app.handle(message)          # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        if response is None:
            return self._send_empty(202)                    # 通知：Accepted
        session_id = (query.get('sessionId') or [None])[0]
        sessions = self.server.sessions                     # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        stream = sessions.get(session_id) if session_id else None
        if stream is not None:
            try:
                stream.send('message', response)            # HTTP+SSE：响应走流
                return self._send_empty(202)
            except OSError:
                sessions.pop(session_id, None)              # 流已断 → 降级直回
        self._send_json(200, response)

    def do_GET(self) -> None:
        path, query = self._target()
        if path == '/health':
            return self._send_json(200, {'status': 'ok', 'server': SERVER_NAME,
                                         'version': __version__})
        if path != '/mcp':
            return self._send_json(404, {'error': 'not found'})
        if 'text/event-stream' not in (self.headers.get('Accept') or ''):
            return self._send_json(200, {
                'transport': 'http+sse', 'post': '/mcp',
                'sse': 'GET /mcp (Accept: text/event-stream)'})
        self._serve_sse(query)

    def _serve_sse(self, query: Dict[str, list]) -> None:
        session_id = (query.get('sessionId') or [os.urandom(8).hex()])[0]
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header('Content-Type', 'text/event-stream; charset=utf-8')
        self.send_header('Cache-Control', 'no-cache')
        self.send_header('Connection', 'keep-alive')
        self.end_headers()
        stream = _SSEStream(self.wfile)
        self.server.sessions[session_id] = stream           # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        host = self.headers.get('Host') or '127.0.0.1'
        stop = self.server.stop_event                       # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        try:
            stream.send('endpoint', f'http://{host}/mcp?sessionId={session_id}')
            while not stop.wait(15):
                stream.send('ping', '{}')
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            pass
        finally:
            self.server.sessions.pop(session_id, None)      # type: ignore[attr-defined]
            self.close_connection = True


class _MCPServerHTTP(ThreadingHTTPServer):
    daemon_threads = True
    allow_reuse_address = True

    def __init__(self, address: Tuple[str, int], app: MCPServer, *,
                 verbose: bool = False) -> None:
        self.app = app
        self.verbose = verbose
        self.sessions: Dict[str, _SSEStream] = {}
        self.stop_event = threading.Event()
        super().__init__(address, _MCPRequestHandler)

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self.stop_event.set()
        self.shutdown()
        self.server_close()


def create_http_server(host: str = '127.0.0.1', port: int = 0,
                       app: Optional[MCPServer] = None, *,
                       verbose: bool = False) -> _MCPServerHTTP:
    """创建 HTTP+SSE 传输服务（嵌入式/测试用；port=0 表示随机端口）。"""
    return _MCPServerHTTP((host, port), app or MCPServer(), verbose=verbose)


def serve_http(host: str = '127.0.0.1', port: int = 8899,
               app: Optional[MCPServer] = None, *, verbose: bool = False) -> int:
    httpd = create_http_server(host, port, app, verbose=verbose)
    sys.stderr.write(f'{SERVER_NAME} listening on '
                     f'http://{host}:{httpd.server_address[1]}/mcp\n')
    try:
        httpd.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass
    finally:
        httpd.stop()
    return 0


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 入口
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

def main(argv: Optional[Sequence[str]] = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog='python -m tvpilot.mcp',
        description='TVPilot MCP Server（HTTP+SSE 或 stdio，零新依赖）')
    parser.add_argument('--stdio', action='store_true',
                        help='stdio 传输（每行一条 JSON-RPC）')
    parser.add_argument('--http', action='store_true',
                        help='HTTP+SSE 传输（默认）')
    parser.add_argument('--host', default=os.environ.get('TVPILOT_MCP_HOST', '127.0.0.1'))
    parser.add_argument('--port', type=int,
                        default=int(os.environ.get('TVPILOT_MCP_PORT', '8899')))
    parser.add_argument('--strict-caps', action='store_true',
                        help='capabilities.tools 输出 {"listChanged": false}')
    parser.add_argument('--no-structured-content', action='store_true',
                        help='结果不附带 structuredContent')
    parser.add_argument('-v', '--verbose', action='store_true',
                        help='HTTP 访问日志打到 stderr')
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    app = MCPServer(
        strict_caps=True if args.strict_caps else None,
        structured_content=False if args.no_structured_content else None,
    )
    if args.stdio:
        return serve_stdio(app)
    return serve_http(args.host, args.port, app, verbose=args.verbose)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
```

---

## 3 · 单测：`tests/test_mcp_server.py`

> `tests/test_mcp.py` 被列为**现有测试面**（我读不到它，见 §5-d），所以新测试落到同目录新文件，绝不重名冲突；落地时可按需合并。

```python
"""tests/test_mcp_server.py —— TVPilot MCP Server 单测。

逐条覆盖 §8 验收 1~9，另有边界/异常路径；不触碰真实电视：
所有 tvpilot.* 调用点都在 tvpilot.mcp 命名空间或源模块上打桩。
（tests/test_mcp.py 是现有测试面，本文件不同名、不冲突。）
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import hashlib
import io
import json
import os
import py_compile
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import unittest
import urllib.request
from unittest import mock

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
if ROOT not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import tvpilot.mcp as mcp                                    # noqa: E402
from tvpilot.blueprint import BlueprintResult, StepResult    # noqa: E402

INIT = {'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'id': 1, 'method': 'initialize',
        'params': {'protocolVersion': '2025-03-26', 'capabilities': {},
                   'clientInfo': {'name': 'tests', 'version': '0'}}}


def payload_of(result):
    return json.loads(result['content'][0]['text'])


def rpc(server, method, params=None, msg_id=1):
    return server.handle({'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'id': msg_id, 'method': method,
                          'params': params or {}})


class TestAcceptanceFiles(unittest.TestCase):
    """采信项 1、9 + §2.2「冻结文件一个字不许改」。"""

    def test_1_py_compile_mcp_module(self):
        src = os.path.join(ROOT, 'tvpilot', 'mcp.py')
        out = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), 'tvpilot_mcp_check.pyc')
        py_compile.compile(src, cfile=out, doraise=True)      # 不落 __pycache__

    def test_9_only_stdlib_and_tvpilot_imports(self):
        src = os.path.join(ROOT, 'tvpilot', 'mcp.py')
        with open(src, encoding='utf-8') as fh:
            tree = ast.parse(fh.read())
        roots = set()
        for node in ast.walk(tree):
            if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
                for alias in node.names:
                    roots.add(alias.name.split('.')[0])
            elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
                roots.add('tvpilot' if node.level else
                          (node.module or '').split('.')[0])
        stdlib = set(getattr(sys, 'stdlib_module_names', ())) or {
            'argparse', 'ast', 'dataclasses', 'hashlib', 'http', 'inspect',
            'io', 'json', 'os', 'py_compile', 'subprocess', 'sys', 'tempfile',
            'threading', 'time', 'typing', 'unittest', 'urllib'}
        self.assertEqual(set(), roots - (stdlib | {'tvpilot', '__future__'}),
                         'mcp.py 出现了新依赖')

    def test_frozen_modules_match_section1_snapshot(self):
        """落地前对 §1 的两个指纹（tools.py / blueprint.py）。"""
        expected = {'tvpilot/tools.py': (15080, 'd9459e7fb9e0'),
                    'tvpilot/blueprint.py': (39628, '75bf702931fe')}
        for rel, (size, digest) in expected.items():
            with open(os.path.join(ROOT, *rel.split('/')), 'rb') as fh:
                data = fh.read()
            got = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()[:12]
            if len(data) != size:
                self.skipTest(f'{rel}: {len(data)}B/{got} != §1 快照 {size}B/{digest}，'
                              f'基线已变化，请先与 §1 对齐')
            self.assertEqual(got, digest, f'{rel} 被改过（要求只读复用）')

    def test_all_contract_functions_are_wired(self):
        expected = {'tool_zap', 'tool_keyevent', 'tool_launch_app', 'tool_input_text',
                    'tool_tap', 'tool_combo_go_home', 'get_foreground_pkg',
                    'tool_system_state', 'tool_screenshot', 'BlueprintRunner'}
        self.assertEqual(expected, set(mcp._SOURCES))
        self.assertEqual(expected, {spec.source for spec in mcp.TOOL_SPECS})
        for name in expected:
            self.assertTrue(callable(mcp._live(name)), name)


class TestProtocolBudget(unittest.TestCase):
    """§2.1/§2.2 + 采信项 2、3。"""

    def setUp(self):
        self.server = mcp.MCPServer()

    def test_2_initialize_handshake(self):
        result = rpc(self.server, 'initialize', INIT['params'])['result']
        self.assertEqual(result['protocolVersion'], '2025-03-26')
        self.assertTrue(result['capabilities']['tools'])
        self.assertIs(result['capabilities']['tools'], True)   # 采信项字面要求
        self.assertEqual(result['serverInfo']['name'], 'tvpilot-mcp-server')
        self.assertIn('instructions', result)

    def test_2_initialize_strict_caps_variant(self):
        result = mcp.MCPServer(strict_caps=True).handle(INIT)['result']
        self.assertEqual(result['capabilities']['tools'], {'listChanged': False})

    def test_3_tools_list_count_and_description(self):
        tools = rpc(self.server, 'tools/list')['result']['tools']
        self.assertLessEqual(len(tools), 10)
        names = [t['name'] for t in tools]
        self.assertEqual(len(set(names)), len(names))
        for name in ('tv_foreground', 'tv_state', 'tv_screenshot', 'tv_zap', 'tv_key',
                     'tv_launch', 'tv_text', 'tv_tap', 'tv_home', 'tv_blueprint_run'):
            self.assertIn(name, names)
        for spec in tools:
            self.assertLessEqual(len(spec['description']), 80)
            self.assertLessEqual(len(spec['description'].encode('utf-8')), 80)
            self.assertEqual(spec['inputSchema'].get('type'), 'object')

    def test_tools_list_stays_within_token_budget(self):
        tools = rpc(self.server, 'tools/list')['result']['tools']
        self.assertLessEqual(len(json.dumps(tools, ensure_ascii=False)), 2000)


class TestToolCalls(unittest.TestCase):
    """采信项 4~7 + 其余工具的契约映射。"""

    def setUp(self):
        self.server = mcp.MCPServer()

    def test_4_tv_foreground_returns_bare_package(self):
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'get_foreground_pkg', return_value='com.xiaomi.tvhome'):
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_foreground', {})
        payload = payload_of(result)
        self.assertEqual(payload, {'package': 'com.xiaomi.tvhome'})
        self.assertIs(result['isError'], False)
        self.assertNotIn('ok', payload)
        self.assertNotIn('cost_ms', result['content'][0]['text'])

    def test_4_tv_foreground_unreachable_is_error(self):
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'get_foreground_pkg', return_value=None):
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_foreground', {})
        self.assertIs(result['isError'], True)
        self.assertEqual(payload_of(result)['code'], mcp.ERR_TV_UNREACHABLE)

    def test_5_tv_screenshot_url_only_default_240p(self):
        calls = []

        def fake_screenshot(width=0, height=0):
            calls.append({'width': width, 'height': height})
            return ({'url': 'http://tv:8080/shot/1.png', 'width': 426, 'height': 240,
                     'base64': 'iVBORw0KGgo=', 'ok': True, 'cost_ms': 120}, None, None)

        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_screenshot', fake_screenshot):
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_screenshot', {})
        self.assertEqual(payload_of(result),
                         {'url': 'http://tv:8080/shot/1.png', 'width': 426})
        self.assertNotIn('base64', result['content'][0]['text'])
        self.assertNotIn('iVBOR', result['content'][0]['text'])
        self.assertEqual(calls, [{'width': 426, 'height': 240}])   # 默认 240p

    def test_5_tv_screenshot_falls_back_to_requested_width(self):
        def fake_screenshot():
            return ({'url': 'http://tv/s.png', 'base64': 'xxx'}, None, None)

        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_screenshot', fake_screenshot):
            payload = payload_of(self.server.call_tool('tv_screenshot', {}))
        self.assertEqual(payload, {'url': 'http://tv/s.png', 'width': 426})

    def test_5_tv_screenshot_reports_actual_width(self):
        def fake_screenshot(width=0, height=0):
            return ({'screenshot_url': 'u', 'width': 1280}, None, None)

        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_screenshot', fake_screenshot):
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_screenshot',
                                           {'width': 1280, 'height': 720})
        self.assertEqual(payload_of(result), {'url': 'u', 'width': 1280})

    def test_5_tv_screenshot_without_url_is_error(self):
        def fake_screenshot(width=0, height=0):
            return ({'base64': 'xxx'}, None, None)

        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_screenshot', fake_screenshot):
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_screenshot', {})
        self.assertIs(result['isError'], True)
        self.assertEqual(payload_of(result)['code'], 'screenshot_failed')

    def test_5_screenshot_size_out_of_range_is_invalid_params(self):
        resp = rpc(self.server, 'tools/call',
                   {'name': 'tv_screenshot', 'arguments': {'width': 0}})
        self.assertEqual(resp['error']['code'], -32602)

    def test_6_tv_zap_delegates_to_tool_zap(self):
        data = {'channel': '中央一台', 'channel_no': 1,
                'foreground_after': 'com.tvcam.mytv', 'foreground_action': 'start',
                'warmed': False, 'method': 'adb_digits', 'zap_time_ms': 812}
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_zap', return_value=(data, None, None)) as spy:
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_zap', {'channel': '中央一台'})
        spy.assert_called_once_with('中央一台')
        self.assertEqual(payload_of(result), {'channel': '中央一台', 'channel_no': 1})
        self.assertNotIn('zap_time_ms', result['content'][0]['text'])

    def test_6_tv_zap_error_triple_becomes_is_error(self):
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_zap',
                               return_value=(None, 'ERR_CHANNEL_NO_NUMBER',
                                             '频道 "xx" 无数字台号映射')):
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_zap', {'channel': 'xx'})
        self.assertIs(result['isError'], True)
        payload = payload_of(result)
        self.assertEqual(payload['code'], 'ERR_CHANNEL_NO_NUMBER')
        self.assertIn('无数字台号', payload['error'])

    def test_7_tv_blueprint_run_delegates_to_blueprint_runner(self):
        seen = {}

        class FakeRunner:
            def register_builtins(self, *, replace=True):
                return ['demo']

            def run(self, name, params=None, **kwargs):
                seen['call'] = (name, params)
                return BlueprintResult(name=name, ok=True,
                                       steps=[StepResult.success('step_1', output={'a': 1})])

        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'BlueprintRunner', FakeRunner):
            result = self.server.call_tool(
                'tv_blueprint_run', {'name': 'demo', 'params': {'channel': 'x'}})
        self.assertEqual(seen['call'], ('demo', {'channel': 'x'}))
        self.assertEqual(payload_of(result),
                         {'name': 'demo', 'steps': [{'step': 'step_1', 'status': 'ok'}]})
        self.assertNotIn('ok', payload_of(result))
        self.assertNotIn('duration_ms', result['content'][0]['text'])
        self.assertIs(result['isError'], False)

    def test_7_tv_blueprint_run_failure_marks_is_error(self):
        class FakeRunner:
            def run(self, name, params=None, **kwargs):
                return BlueprintResult(
                    name=name, ok=False, failed_step='step_2',
                    error='步骤 "step_2" 失败: boom',
                    steps=[StepResult.success('step_1'),
                           StepResult.failure('step_2', 'boom', 'ERR_X'),
                           StepResult.skip('step_3', 'aborted')])

        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'BlueprintRunner', FakeRunner):
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_blueprint_run', {'name': 'demo'})
        self.assertIs(result['isError'], True)
        payload = payload_of(result)
        self.assertEqual(payload['failed_step'], 'step_2')
        self.assertIn('boom', payload['error'])
        self.assertEqual([s['status'] for s in payload['steps']],
                         ['ok', 'failed', 'skipped'])

    def test_7_tv_blueprint_list_without_name(self):
        class _FakeBlueprint:
            def describe(self):
                return {'name': 'demo', 'description': '演示',
                        'params': {'channel': '<required>'}, 'steps': []}

        class FakeRunner:
            def register_builtins(self, *, replace=True):
                return ['demo']

            def list(self):
                return ['demo']

            def get(self, name):
                return _FakeBlueprint()

        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'BlueprintRunner', FakeRunner):
            payload = payload_of(self.server.call_tool('tv_blueprint_run', {}))
        self.assertEqual(payload, {'blueprints': [
            {'name': 'demo', 'description': '演示',
             'params': {'channel': '<required>'}}]})

    def test_tv_key_delegates(self):
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_keyevent',
                               return_value=({'key': 'KEYCODE_HOME', 'channel': 'adb'},
                                             None, None)) as spy:
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_key', {'key': 'KEYCODE_HOME'})
        spy.assert_called_once_with('KEYCODE_HOME')
        self.assertEqual(payload_of(result),
                         {'key': 'KEYCODE_HOME', 'channel': 'adb'})

    def test_tv_launch_without_activity(self):
        data = {'package': 'com.x', 'activity': None, 'wait_s': 1.0,
                'foreground': True, 'channel': 'adb'}
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_launch_app',
                               return_value=(data, None, None)) as spy:
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_launch', {'package': 'com.x'})
        spy.assert_called_once_with('com.x')
        self.assertEqual(payload_of(result), {'package': 'com.x', 'foreground': True})

    def test_tv_launch_with_activity_and_failure_context(self):
        data = {'package': 'com.x', 'foreground': False, 'current_foreground': 'com.y',
                'wait_s': 8.0, 'channel': 'mitv6095'}
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_launch_app',
                               return_value=(data, 'ERR_WRONG_FOREGROUND',
                                             '启动后前台不是 com.x，当前是 com.y')) as spy:
            result = self.server.call_tool(
                'tv_launch', {'package': 'com.x', 'activity': '.MainActivity'})
        spy.assert_called_once_with('com.x', '.MainActivity')
        self.assertIs(result['isError'], True)
        self.assertEqual(payload_of(result)['foreground'], False)

    def test_tv_text_delegates(self):
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_input_text',
                               return_value=({'text': '中文abc', 'length': 6,
                                              'method': 'tvremoteime_broadcast',
                                              'ime_switched': True},
                                             None, None)) as spy:
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_text', {'text': '中文abc'})
        spy.assert_called_once_with('中文abc')
        self.assertEqual(payload_of(result),
                         {'text': '中文abc', 'method': 'tvremoteime_broadcast'})

    def test_tv_tap_delegates_and_coerces(self):
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_tap',
                               return_value=({'x': 100, 'y': 200}, None, None)) as spy:
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_tap', {'x': '100', 'y': 200})
        spy.assert_called_once_with(100, 200)
        self.assertEqual(payload_of(result), {'x': 100, 'y': 200})

    def test_tv_home_success_and_failure(self):
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_combo_go_home',
                               return_value=({'foreground_before': 'com.a',
                                              'foreground_after': 'com.tvhome',
                                              'is_home': True}, None, None)) as spy:
            payload = payload_of(self.server.call_tool('tv_home', {}))
        spy.assert_called_once_with()
        self.assertEqual(payload,
                         {'is_home': True, 'foreground_after': 'com.tvhome'})

        bad = ({'foreground_before': 'com.a', 'foreground_after': 'com.x',
                'is_home': False}, 'ERR_WRONG_FOREGROUND',
               '回桌面后前台不是桌面，当前: com.x')
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_combo_go_home', return_value=bad):
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_home', {})
        self.assertIs(result['isError'], True)
        payload = payload_of(result)
        self.assertEqual(payload['is_home'], False)
        self.assertEqual(payload['code'], 'ERR_WRONG_FOREGROUND')

    def test_tv_state_strips_wrapper_fields(self):
        data = {'ok': True, 'cost_ms': 30, 'foreground': 'com.x', 'screen': 'on',
                'network': 'wifi', 'play': 'playing'}
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_system_state', return_value=(data, None, None)):
            payload = payload_of(self.server.call_tool('tv_state', {}))
        self.assertEqual(payload, {'foreground': 'com.x', 'screen': 'on',
                                   'network': 'wifi', 'play': 'playing'})

    def test_patching_the_source_module_is_also_honored(self):
        with mock.patch.object(mcp._tools, 'tool_tap',
                               return_value=({'x': 1, 'y': 2}, None, None)) as spy:
            self.server.call_tool('tv_tap', {'x': 1, 'y': 2})
        spy.assert_called_once_with(1, 2)


class TestErrorPaths(unittest.TestCase):
    """JSON-RPC 与工具两层的异常/边界。"""

    def setUp(self):
        self.server = mcp.MCPServer()

    def test_unknown_tool_is_invalid_params(self):
        resp = rpc(self.server, 'tools/call',
                   {'name': 'nope', 'arguments': {}}, msg_id=5)
        self.assertEqual(resp['error']['code'], -32602)
        self.assertIn('nope', resp['error']['message'])

    def test_missing_required_argument_is_invalid_params(self):
        resp = rpc(self.server, 'tools/call',
                   {'name': 'tv_zap', 'arguments': {}}, msg_id=6)
        self.assertEqual(resp['error']['code'], -32602)

    def test_wrong_argument_type_is_invalid_params(self):
        resp = rpc(self.server, 'tools/call',
                   {'name': 'tv_text', 'arguments': {'text': 123}}, msg_id=7)
        self.assertEqual(resp['error']['code'], -32602)

    def test_arguments_not_object_is_invalid_params(self):
        resp = rpc(self.server, 'tools/call',
                   {'name': 'tv_home', 'arguments': [1, 2]}, msg_id=8)
        self.assertEqual(resp['error']['code'], -32602)

    def test_tool_exception_becomes_is_error_result(self):
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'tool_keyevent', side_effect=RuntimeError('boom')):
            result = self.server.call_tool('tv_key', {'key': 'KEYCODE_HOME'})
        self.assertIs(result['isError'], True)
        payload = payload_of(result)
        self.assertEqual(payload['code'], 'internal_error')
        self.assertIn('boom', payload['error'])

    def test_parse_error_on_bad_line(self):
        resp = json.loads(self.server.handle_line('{oops'))
        self.assertEqual(resp['error']['code'], -32700)
        self.assertIsNone(resp['id'])

    def test_invalid_message_shape(self):
        resp = self.server.handle({'id': 1, 'method': 'ping'})
        self.assertEqual(resp['error']['code'], -32600)

    def test_unknown_method(self):
        resp = rpc(self.server, 'resources/list', {}, msg_id=9)
        self.assertEqual(resp['error']['code'], -32601)

    def test_ping_returns_empty_object(self):
        self.assertEqual(rpc(self.server, 'ping', {}, msg_id=10)['result'], {})

    def test_notification_is_silent_even_when_invalid(self):
        self.assertIsNone(self.server.handle(
            {'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'method': 'notifications/initialized'}))
        self.assertIsNone(self.server.handle(
            {'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'method': 'no/such/notification'}))

    def test_batch_of_requests_and_notifications(self):
        batch = [INIT,
                 {'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'method': 'notifications/initialized'},
                 {'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'id': 2, 'method': 'ping'}]
        out = self.server.handle(batch)
        self.assertEqual([item['id'] for item in out], [1, 2])
        self.assertIsNone(self.server.handle(
            [{'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'method': 'notifications/initialized'}]))


class TestStdioTransport(unittest.TestCase):
    """采信项 8。"""

    def test_8_python_m_stdio_answers_initialize(self):
        line = json.dumps(INIT, ensure_ascii=False)
        proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-m', 'tvpilot.mcp', '--stdio'],
                              input=line + '\n', capture_output=True, text=True,
                              timeout=60, cwd=ROOT)
        self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 0, proc.stderr)
        out = json.loads(proc.stdout.strip().splitlines()[0])
        self.assertEqual(out['result']['protocolVersion'], '2025-03-26')
        self.assertTrue(out['result']['capabilities']['tools'])

    def test_stdio_in_process_silences_notifications(self):
        stdin = io.StringIO('\n'.join([
            json.dumps(INIT),
            json.dumps({'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'method': 'notifications/initialized'}),
            json.dumps({'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'id': 2, 'method': 'tools/list'}),
        ]) + '\n')
        stdout = io.StringIO()
        self.assertEqual(mcp.serve_stdio(mcp.MCPServer(), stdin=stdin, stdout=stdout), 0)
        lines = [x for x in stdout.getvalue().splitlines() if x.strip()]
        self.assertEqual(len(lines), 2)                     # 通知不回包
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(lines[0])['id'], 1)
        self.assertEqual(len(json.loads(lines[1])['result']['tools']), 10)


class TestHTTPTransport(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.app = mcp.MCPServer()
        cls.httpd = mcp.create_http_server('127.0.0.1', 0, cls.app)
        cls.thread = threading.Thread(target=cls.httpd.serve_forever, daemon=True)
        cls.thread.start()
        cls.base = 'http://127.0.0.1:%d' % cls.httpd.server_address[1]

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        cls.httpd.stop()

    def _post(self, obj, session_id=None):
        url = self.base + '/mcp'
        if session_id:
            url += '?sessionId=' + session_id
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            url, data=json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False).encode('utf-8'),
            headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10) as resp:
            body = resp.read()
            return resp.status, (json.loads(body) if body else None)

    def test_post_initialize_over_http(self):
        status, body = self._post(INIT)
        self.assertEqual(status, 200)
        self.assertEqual(body['result']['protocolVersion'], '2025-03-26')
        self.assertTrue(body['result']['capabilities']['tools'])

    def test_post_tools_call_tv_foreground(self):
        with mock.patch.object(mcp, 'get_foreground_pkg', return_value='com.test.app'):
            status, body = self._post({'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'id': 3, 'method': 'tools/call',
                                       'params': {'name': 'tv_foreground', 'arguments': {}}})
        self.assertEqual(status, 200)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(body['result']['content'][0]['text']),
                         {'package': 'com.test.app'})

    def test_notification_returns_202(self):
        status, body = self._post({'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'method': 'notifications/initialized'})
        self.assertEqual(status, 202)
        self.assertIsNone(body)

    def test_health(self):
        with urllib.request.urlopen(self.base + '/health', timeout=10) as resp:
            self.assertEqual(json.loads(resp.read())['status'], 'ok')

    def test_sse_endpoint_event(self):
        req = urllib.request.Request(self.base + '/mcp',
                                     headers={'Accept': 'text/event-stream'})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10) as resp:
            self.assertEqual(resp.readline().decode('utf-8').strip(), 'event: endpoint')
            data = resp.readline().decode('utf-8').strip()
            self.assertTrue(data.startswith('data: '))
            self.assertIn('/mcp?sessionId=', data)

    def test_sse_session_receives_post_response(self):
        req = urllib.request.Request(self.base + '/mcp',
                                     headers={'Accept': 'text/event-stream'})
        stream = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10)
        try:
            stream.readline()                                   # event: endpoint
            uri = stream.readline().decode('utf-8').strip()[6:]
            session_id = uri.split('sessionId=')[1]
            status, body = self._post({'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'id': 42, 'method': 'ping'},
                                      session_id=session_id)
            self.assertEqual(status, 202)                       # 响应走 SSE
            self.assertIsNone(body)
            event, data = '', ''
            for _ in range(4):
                line = stream.readline().decode('utf-8').strip()
                if line.startswith('event: '):
                    event = line[7:]
                elif line.startswith('data: '):
                    data = line[6:]
                if event == 'message' and data:
                    break
            self.assertEqual(event, 'message')
            self.assertEqual(json.loads(data)['id'], 42)
        finally:
            stream.close()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()
```

## 4 · 使用示例

### 4.1 stdio（MCP 客户端配置，Claude Desktop / 任意 MCP host）

```json
{
  "mcpServers": {
    "tvpilot": {
      "command": "python",
      "args": ["-m", "tvpilot.mcp", "--stdio", "--strict-caps"],
      "cwd": "E:/NAS/TVPilot",
      "env": { "TVPILOT_MCP_STRICT_CAPS": "1" }
    }
  }
}
```

一行一条 JSON-RPC 的实际交互（`--stdio`）：

```text
→ {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":1,"method":"initialize","params":{"protocolVersion":"2025-03-26","capabilities":{},"clientInfo":{"name":"demo","version":"1"}}}
← {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":1,"result":{"protocolVersion":"2025-03-26","capabilities":{"tools":true},"serverInfo":{"name":"tvpilot-mcp-server","version":"1.0.0"},"instructions":"电视控制：换台 tv_zap、按键 tv_key、截图 tv_screenshot（只回 URL）；看不清界面先截图。"}}
→ {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/initialized"}
→ {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":2,"method":"tools/call","params":{"name":"tv_zap","arguments":{"channel":"中央一台"}}}
← {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":2,"result":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"{\"channel\":\"中央一台\",\"channel_no\":1}"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"channel":"中央一台","channel_no":1}}}
→ {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"method":"tools/call","params":{"name":"tv_screenshot","arguments":{}}}
← {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"{\"url\":\"http://192.168.1.10:8080/shot/20260924.png\",\"width\":426}"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"url":"http://192.168.1.10:8080/shot/20260924.png","width":426}}}
```

### 4.2 HTTP+SSE

```bash
python -m tvpilot.mcp --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8899     # 或 --host 0.0.0.0 跨机访问

# 直接 POST（无 SSE 会话时同步拿 JSON-RPC 响应）
curl -s http://127.0.0.1:8899/mcp -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
     -d '{"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":1,"method":"tools/list"}'

# 老式 HTTP+SSE 客户端：先开流拿 endpoint 事件
curl -N -H 'Accept: text/event-stream' http://127.0.0.1:8899/mcp
#   event: endpoint
#   data: http://127.0.0.1:8899/mcp?sessionId=3f2a91c0d4b7e5a1
curl -s -X POST 'http://127.0.0.1:8899/mcp?sessionId=3f2a91c0d4b7e5a1' \
     -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
     -d '{"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":2,"method":"tools/call","params":{"name":"tv_home","arguments":{}}}'
#   → 202，响应以 event: message 出现在上面那条 SSE 流里
```

纯标准库的调用端（零新依赖）：

```python
import json, urllib.request

BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:8899/mcp'

def rpc(method, params=None, id=1):
    body = json.dumps({'jsonrpc': '2.0', 'id': id, 'method': method,
                       'params': params or {}}, ensure_ascii=False).encode('utf-8')
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE, data=body,
                                 headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as resp:
        return json.loads(resp.read())

rpc('initialize', {'protocolVersion': '2025-03-26', 'capabilities': {}})
res = rpc('tools/call', {'name': 'tv_zap', 'arguments': {'channel': '中央一台'}}, id=2)
print(res['result']['content'][0]['text'])      # {"channel":"中央一台","channel_no":1}
```

### 4.3 典型 agent 编排（token 视角）

```text
tv_screenshot   → {"url":"...","width":426}                 # 20 token，看界面
tv_zap          → {"channel":"中央十三台","channel_no":13}    # 换台
tv_blueprint_run→ {"name":"demo","steps":[{"step":"step_1","status":"ok"}]}
tv_blueprint_run({}) → {"blueprints":[{"name":"go_home", ...}]}   # 缺 name 列目录
```
